Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The regular variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 209 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 795 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the regular decomposition. By minimality, the element a^209 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 795-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
